Toontown: Anywhere Quest Guide

Every ToonTask in the game, in the order the game gives them: 1061 task chains and 1305 steps across 50 tiers, read straight out of the game's own quest data.

How the quest line works

  • The game is split into tiers. Each tier has a list of required rewards (Laff boosts, gag training, pouches, cog suit parts…). Earn all of them and you move to the next tier.
  • When you ask Toon HQ or a Toon for a task, you are offered up to three. Each is tied to one of the rewards you still need, or to a bonus "just for fun" reward.
  • A task chain has one or more steps. You only get the reward after the last step; the steps in between are just errands that lead there.
  • Where a step lists several next steps, the game picks one. The open task pool is a shared list of chains that can be given for any reward in that tier.
  • Tasks marked HELPER are for helping a Toon with a low Laff meter, and are only offered to Toons who qualify.

Colours

WANTED / DEFEAT fight Cogs · DELIVER / RECOVER / VISIT errands · FRIEND / TROLLEY / CLARABELLE social · CHOOSE pick a gag track

Toontown Central Tiers 0–3

Tasks are handed to you one at a time.

    Clarabelle Call Clarabelle
    Get it from any Toon HQ · Hand in to any Toon HQ · Reward: 1 point Laff boost
    What they say

    Did you know you have your very own Toon house?

    Clarabelle Cow runs a phone catalog where you can order furniture to decorate your house.

    You can also buy SpeedChat phrases, clothing, and other fun things!

    I'll tell Clarabelle to send you your first catalog now.

    You get a catalog with new items every week!

    Go to your home and use your phone to call Clarabelle.

Tier 1 1 required reward · 2 task chains

Each time you ask for a task you are offered up to 2 to pick from.

Garden Grove Tier 7

Tier 7 9 required rewards · 70 task chains

Each time you ask for a task you are offered up to 3 to pick from.

Rewards you must earn to finish this tier (in this order)

  1. 1 point Laff boost
  2. Small Bag (35)
  3. 2 point Laff boost
  4. 3 point Laff boost
  5. 1 point Laff boost
  6. 2 point Laff boost
  7. Carry 3 ToonTasks
  8. Carry 80 jellybeans
  9. Teleport access to Garden Grove

Just for fun rewards (extra tasks, not needed to move on)

Clothing Ticket · 60 jellybeans ×2 · 100 jellybeans ×2 · Big head · Small head · Big legs · Small legs · Big toon · Small toon

Set task chains (each ends in a particular reward)

Tier 9 23 required rewards · 72 task chains

Each time you ask for a task you are offered up to 3 to pick from.

Rewards you must earn to finish this tier (in this order)

  1. 1 point Laff boost
  2. Gag training, frame 1 of your chosen track
  3. Gag training, frame 2 of your chosen track
  4. Medium Bag (40)
  5. Gag training, frame 3 of your chosen track
  6. Gag training, frame 4 of your chosen track
  7. 2 point Laff boost
  8. Gag training, frame 5 of your chosen track
  9. Gag training, frame 6 of your chosen track
  10. 3 point Laff boost
  11. Gag training, frame 7 of your chosen track
  12. Gag training, frame 8 of your chosen track
  13. 1 point Laff boost
  14. Gag training, frame 9 of your chosen track
  15. Gag training, frame 10 of your chosen track
  16. 2 point Laff boost
  17. Gag training, frame 11 of your chosen track
  18. Gag training, frame 12 of your chosen track
  19. Carry 100 jellybeans
  20. Gag training, frame 13 of your chosen track
  21. Gag training, frame 14 of your chosen track
  22. Gag training, frame 15 of your chosen track
  23. Teleport access to Melody Meadow

Slumber Springs Tiers 14–17

Tier 14 10 required rewards · 105 task chains

Each time you ask for a task you are offered up to 3 to pick from.

Rewards you must earn to finish this tier (in this order)

  1. Cashbot Upper Left Leg Part
  2. 1 point Laff boost
  3. Small Backpack (60)
  4. 2 point Laff boost
  5. 3 point Laff boost
  6. Carry 150 jellybeans
  7. 4 point Laff boost
  8. Teleport access to Slumber Springs
  9. Cashbot Lower Left Leg Part
  10. Cashbot Left Foot Part

Just for fun rewards (extra tasks, not needed to move on)

250 jellybeans ×4 · 300 jellybeans ×4 · Big head · Small head · Flat portrait · Flat profile · Transparent · No color · Invisible toon

Tier 18 1 required reward · 2 task chains

Each time you ask for a task you are offered up to 3 to pick from.

Rewards you must earn to finish this tier (in this order)

  1. Lawbot Upper Left Leg Part

Set task chains (each ends in a particular reward)

Ends with: Lawbot Upper Left Leg Part
Visit Visit Professor Flake 2 steps · from any Toon that offers tasks (Toon HQ officers do)
  • 1
    Visit Visit Professor Flake
    Get it from any Toon that offers tasks (Toon HQ officers do) · Precipitation Foundation, Polar Place (The Brrrgh)
    What they say

    If you are interested in Lawbot disguise parts you should visit Professor Flake.

    I hear he could use some help with his weather research. (Precipitation Foundation, Polar Place (The Brrrgh))

  • 2
    Recover Recover an External Temperature Sensor from The Cogs on Silly Street
    Get it from Professor Flake Precipitation Foundation, Polar Place (The Brrrgh) · Hand in to Professor Flake Precipitation Foundation, Polar Place (The Brrrgh) · Reward: Lawbot Upper Left Leg Part
    What they say

    Yes, yes. I have Lawbot disguise parts.

    But they are of no interest to me.

    The focus of my research is fluctuations in the ambient temperature of Toontown.

    I will gladly trade you disguise parts for cog temperature sensors.

    You can start on Silly Street.

Recover Recover an External Temperature Sensor from The Cogs on Silly Street 1 step · from the Toon who sent you
  • Continues at step 11001 (shown above)
